        outfitStatusLabel.TextSize = 9
        outfitStatusLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        outfitStatusLabel.ZIndex = 9

        local function applyOutfitByIndex(index)
            local outfit = OUTFITS[index]
            if not outfit then return false end
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return false end

            local ok = false
            if outfit.customApply then
                ok = outfit.customApply(char) == true
            else
                if not _G.Lust.OriginalOutfit.shirt and not _G.Lust.OriginalOutfit.pants then
                    saveOriginalOutfit(char)
                end
                if #_G.Lust.OriginalAccessories == 0 then
                    saveOriginalAccessories(char)
                end

                clearAllOutfit(char)
                clearAllAccessories(char)

                local packName = "__custom_" .. index
                BLEED_PACKS[packName] = {
                    accessory = outfit.accessory,
                    offset = outfit.offset,
                    shirt = outfit.shirt,
                    pants = outfit.pants,
                    headMesh = outfit.headMesh,
                    headTexture = outfit.headTexture,
                    korblox = outfit.korblox,
                    headlessKorblox = outfit.headlessKorblox,
                }
                ok = applyBleedOutfit(packName) == true
            end

            if outfitStatusLabel then
                outfitStatusLabel.Text = string.upper(outfit.label) .. (ok and "  ✓ SUCCESS" or "  ✗ FAILED")
                task.delay(1.5, function()
                    if outfitStatusLabel and outfitStatusLabel.Parent then
                        outfitStatusLabel.Text = ""
                    end
                end)
            end

            currentAccessoryPack = outfit.label
            saveAllSettings()
            return ok
        end

        local function updateOutfitSelector(direction)
            currentOutfitIndex = currentOutfitIndex + direction
            if currentOutfitIndex < 1 then currentOutfitIndex = #OUTFITS end
            if currentOutfitIndex > #OUTFITS then currentOutfitIndex = 1 end
            outfitSelectorLabel.Text = OUTFITS[currentOutfitIndex].label
            applyOutfitByIndex(currentOutfitIndex)
        end

        leftBtn.Activated:Connect(function() updateOutfitSelector(-1) end)
        rightBtn.Activated:Connect(function() updateOutfitSelector(1) end)

        for i, outfit in ipairs(OUTFITS) do
            if outfit.label == currentAccessoryPack then
                currentOutfitIndex = i
                outfitSelectorLabel.Text = outfit.label
                break
            end
        end
    end

    -- ============================================================
    -- ADAPT SKIN (below OUTFIT)
    -- ============================================================
    do
        local ADAPT_PRESETS = { "ADAPT", "RED", "BLUE", "GALAXY" }
        local currentAdaptPreset = "ADAPT"

        if _G._AdaptSkin then
            pcall(function()
                local saved = _G._AdaptSkin.GetPreset and _G._AdaptSkin.GetPreset()
                if saved then currentAdaptPreset = saved end
            end)
        end

        local adaptStatusLabel = nil

        -- ADAPT SKIN TOGGLE
        local adaptToggleRow = mkRow(visualPage, 34)
        mkLabel(adaptToggleRow, "Adapt Skin")
        local adaptPill, adaptDot = mkPill(adaptToggleRow, 44)
        local adaptOn = false
        if _G._AdaptSkin then
            pcall(function() adaptOn = _G._AdaptSkin.IsEnabled() == true end)
        end
        local function adaptAnim(state)
            adaptOn = state
            animPill(adaptPill, adaptDot, state)
        end
        adaptAnim(adaptOn)

        local adaptClk = Instance.new("TextButton", adaptPill)
        adaptClk.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        adaptClk.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        adaptClk.Text = ""
        adaptClk.AutoButtonColor = false
        adaptClk.ZIndex = 10
        adaptClk.Activated:Connect(function()
            adaptAnim(not adaptOn)
            if _G._AdaptSkin then
                pcall(function() _G._AdaptSkin.SetEnabled(adaptOn) end)
            end
            saveAllSettings()
        end)

        _G.KZAXAdaptSkinSetState = adaptAnim

        -- ADAPT SKIN SELECTOR ROW (with 3D preview picker)
        local adaptRow = mkRow(visualPage, 30)
        mkLabel(adaptRow, "Adapt Preset")

        local adaptPickHolder = Instance.new("Frame", adaptRow)
        adaptPickHolder.Name = "AdaptSkinPickHolder"
        adaptPickHolder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 8)
        adaptPickHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        adaptPickHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
        adaptPickHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 66, 0, 26)
        adaptPickHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -73, 0.5, -13)
        adaptPickHolder.ClipsDescendants = true
        adaptPickHolder.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", adaptPickHolder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 13)
        local adaptPickStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", adaptPickHolder)
        adaptPickStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        adaptPickStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        adaptPickStroke.Transparency = 0.15

        local adaptPickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", adaptPickHolder)
        adaptPickBtn.Name = "AdaptSkinPickButton"
        adaptPickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        adaptPickBtn.Text = "PICK"
        adaptPickBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        adaptPickBtn.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        adaptPickBtn.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        adaptPickBtn.TextSize = 11
        adaptPickBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        adaptPickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        adaptPickBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        adaptPickBtn.ZIndex = 9
        adaptPickBtn.AutoButtonColor = false

        -- Status label
        local adaptStatusRow = mkRow(visualPage, 22)
        adaptStatusRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        adaptStatusLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", adaptStatusRow)
        adaptStatusLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
        adaptStatusLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        adaptStatusLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        adaptStatusLabel.Text = ""
        adaptStatusLabel.TextColor3 = RED
        adaptStatusLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        adaptStatusLabel.TextSize = 9
        adaptStatusLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        adaptStatusLabel.ZIndex = 9

        -- Picker GUI
        local adaptPickGui = nil
        local adaptJustClosedAt = 0

        local function closeAdaptPicker()
            if adaptPickGui and adaptPickGui.Parent then
                local f = adaptPickGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
                if f then
                    TS:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
                local g = adaptPickGui
                adaptPickGui = nil
                adaptJustClosedAt = tick()
                task.delay(0.25, function() pcall(function() g:Destroy() end) end)
            end
        end

        -- Build a 3D preview card for a preset
        local function makeAdaptPreview(card, presetName)
            local viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame", card)
            viewport.Name = "AdaptPreview"
            viewport.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -26)
            viewport.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 8)
            viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
            viewport.ZIndex = 4
            viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1)
            Instance.new("UICorner", viewport).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

            local world = Instance.new("WorldModel", viewport)

            local built = false
            if _G._AdaptSkin and type(_G._AdaptSkin.CreatePreviewRig) == "function" then
                local ok, rig = pcall(_G._AdaptSkin.CreatePreviewRig, presetName)
                if ok and rig then
                    rig.Parent = world

                    for _, d in ipairs(rig:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                            d.Anchored = true
                            d.CanCollide = false
                            d.CanQuery = false
                            d.CanTouch = false
                            d.Transparency = 0
                            d.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                        end
                    end

                    local head = rig:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if head then
                        head.Transparency = 0
                        head.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                    end

                    local hrp = rig:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        rig.PrimaryPart = hrp
                    else
                        local first = rig:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                        if first then rig.PrimaryPart = first end
                    end

                    if rig.PrimaryPart then
                        local boxCF, boxSize = rig:GetBoundingBox()
                        local focus = boxCF.Position
                        local maxDim = math.max(boxSize.X, boxSize.Y, boxSize.Z)
                        local dist = math.max(maxDim * 1.9, 5.5)

                        local cam = Instance.new("Camera", viewport)
                        cam.FieldOfView = 60
                        viewport.CurrentCamera = cam
                        cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(focus + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, dist), focus)

                        local baseCF = rig.PrimaryPart.CFrame
                        local started = tick()
                        local conn
                        conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                            if not viewport.Parent or not rig.Parent or not rig.PrimaryPart then
                                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                                return
                            end
                            local t = tick() - started
                            local pivot = rig.PrimaryPart.CFrame.Position
                            rig:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pivot) * CFrame.Angles(0, t * 1.4, 0) * (baseCF - pivot))
                        end)
                        built = true
                    end
                end
            end

            if not built then
                local model = Instance.new("Model", world)

                local torso = Instance.new("Part", model)
                torso.Name = "Torso"
                torso.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
                torso.Anchored = true
                torso.CanCollide = false
                torso.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

                local head = Instance.new("Part", model)
                head.Name = "Head"
                head.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
                head.Size = Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2)
                head.Anchored = true
                head.CanCollide = false
                head.Position = Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 0)
                head.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

                model.PrimaryPart = torso

                local focus = Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0)
                local dist = 7
                local cam = Instance.new("Camera", viewport)
                cam.FieldOfView = 60
                viewport.CurrentCamera = cam
                cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(focus + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, dist), focus)

                local base = torso.CFrame
                local started = tick()
                local conn
                conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    if not viewport.Parent or not torso.Parent then
                        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                        return
                    end
                    local t = tick() - started
                    torso.CFrame = base * CFrame.Angles(0, t * 1.4, 0)
                    head.Position = torso.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 0)
                end)
            end
        end

        adaptPickBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if adaptPickGui then closeAdaptPicker(); return end
            if tick() - adaptJustClosedAt < 0.25 then return end

            adaptPickGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            adaptPickGui.Name = "KZAXAdaptSkinPicker"
            adaptPickGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            adaptPickGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            adaptPickGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            adaptPickGui.DisplayOrder = 999999
            adaptPickGui.Parent = gui

            local backdrop = Instance.new("Frame", adaptPickGui)
            backdrop.Name = "Backdrop"
            backdrop.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            backdrop.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            backdrop.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
            backdrop.BorderSizePixel = 0
            backdrop.ZIndex = 1

            local frame = Instance.new("Frame", adaptPickGui)
            frame.Name = "Frame"
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 10)
            frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame.ZIndex = 2
            Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
            local fStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
            fStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90)
            fStroke.Thickness = 1.2
            fStroke.Transparency = 0.3

            local titleBar = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
            titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
            titleBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 15)
            titleBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            titleBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
            titleBar.ZIndex = 3
            Instance.new("UICorner", titleBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", titleBar)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
            title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 0)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Text = "ADAPT SKIN PRESETS"
            title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            title.TextSize = 15
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.ZIndex = 4

            local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", titleBar)
            closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 36)
            closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0, 6)
            closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 28)
            closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
            closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            closeBtn.Text = "X"
            closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            closeBtn.TextSize = 14
            closeBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            closeBtn.ZIndex = 4
            Instance.new("UICorner", closeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
            closeBtn.Activated:Connect(closeAdaptPicker)

            local scrollFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", frame)
            scrollFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -64)
            scrollFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 56)
            scrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            scrollFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            scrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 4
            scrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
            scrollFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            scrollFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            scrollFrame.ZIndex = 3

            local grid = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", scrollFrame)
            grid.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 190)
            grid.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
            grid.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            grid.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            grid.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

            local gridPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", scrollFrame)
            gridPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
            gridPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
            gridPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
            gridPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)

            local cardStrokes = {}
            local function refreshAdaptCards()
                for preset, st in pairs(cardStrokes) do
                    local on = (currentAdaptPreset == preset)
                    st.Color = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                    st.Thickness = on and 2 or 1
                    st.Transparency = on and 0.05 or 0.5
                end
            end

            for i, preset in ipairs(ADAPT_PRESETS) do
                local card = Instance.new("TextButton", scrollFrame)
                card.Name = "Adapt_" .. preset
                card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)
                card.BorderSizePixel = 0
                card.Text = ""
                card.AutoButtonColor = false
                card.LayoutOrder = i
                card.ZIndex = 3
                Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
                local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
                st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                st.Thickness = 1
                st.Transparency = 0.5
                cardStrokes[preset] = st

                makeAdaptPreview(card, preset)

                local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
                nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
                nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -26)
                nameLbl.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
                nameLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
                nameLbl.Text = preset
                nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                nameLbl.TextSize = 12
                nameLbl.TextWrapped = true
                nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                nameLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                nameLbl.ZIndex = 6

                card.Activated:Connect(function()
                    currentAdaptPreset = preset
                    if _G._AdaptSkin then
                        pcall(function() _G._AdaptSkin.SetPreset(preset) end)
                    end
                    refreshAdaptCards()
                    if adaptStatusLabel then
                        adaptStatusLabel.Text = preset .. "  ✓ APPLIED"
                        task.delay(1.5, function()
                            if adaptStatusLabel and adaptStatusLabel.Parent then adaptStatusLabel.Text = "" end
                        end)
                    end
                    saveAllSettings()
                end)
            end
            refreshAdaptCards()

            backdrop.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    closeAdaptPicker()
                end
            end)

            frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            TS:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 400),
                Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0.5, -200)
            }):Play()
        end)

        _G.KZAXAdaptSkinPickBtn = adaptPickBtn
    end

        end
        local csClk = Instance.new("TextButton", csPill)
        csClk.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); csClk.BackgroundTransparency = 1; csClk.Text = ""
        csClk.AutoButtonColor = false; csClk.ZIndex = 10
        csClk.Activated:Connect(function()
            csAnim(not csOn)
            if _G.AmbitiousCustomSounds then
                _G.AmbitiousCustomSounds.setEnabled(csOn)

                end

        end)

        _G.KZAXCustomSoundsSetState = csAnim
        _G.KZAXCustomSoundsToggle = csClk

        -- PICK BUTTON
        local ctPickRow = mkRow(visualPage, 34)
        mkLabel(ctPickRow, "Pick Tool Skin")

        local ctPickHolder = Instance.new("Frame", ctPickRow)
        ctPickHolder.Name = "CustomToolsPickHolder"
        ctPickHolder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 8)
        ctPickHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        ctPickHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ctPickHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 66, 0, 26)
        ctPickHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -73, 0.5, -13)
        ctPickHolder.ClipsDescendants = true
        ctPickHolder.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", ctPickHolder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 13)
        local ctPickStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", ctPickHolder)
        ctPickStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        ctPickStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        ctPickStroke.Transparency = 0.15

        local ctPickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", ctPickHolder)
        ctPickBtn.Name = "CustomToolsPickButton"
        ctPickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ctPickBtn.Text = "PICK"
        ctPickBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        ctPickBtn.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        ctPickBtn.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        ctPickBtn.TextSize = 11
        ctPickBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        ctPickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        ctPickBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ctPickBtn.ZIndex = 9
        ctPickBtn.AutoButtonColor = false

        local ctStatusRow = mkRow(visualPage, 22)
        ctStatusRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ctStatusLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", ctStatusRow)
        ctStatusLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
        ctStatusLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        ctStatusLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1

            if ctPickGui and ctPickGui.Parent then
                local f = ctPickGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
                if f then
                    TS:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                end

                ctJustClosedAt = tick()
                task.delay(0.25, function() pcall(function() g:Destroy() end) end)
            end
        end

        local function makeToolPreview(card, assetKey)
            local assetData = _G.AmbitiousCustomToolAssets and _G.AmbitiousCustomToolAssets[assetKey]
            if not assetData then return end

            local viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame", card)
            viewport.Name = "ToolPreview"
            viewport.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -26)
            viewport.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 8)
            viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
            viewport.ZIndex = 4
            viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1)
            Instance.new("UICorner", viewport).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

            local world = Instance.new("WorldModel", viewport)

            local model = Instance.new("Model", world)
            local part = Instance.new("Part", model)
            part.Name = "PreviewPart"
            part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
            part.Anchored = true
            part.CanCollide = false
            part.CanQuery = false
            part.CanTouch = false
            part.Transparency = 0
            part.Parent = model

            local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh", part)
            mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
            mesh.MeshId = assetData.mesh
            mesh.TextureId = assetData.tex
            mesh.Scale = assetData.scale
            mesh.Parent = part

            model.PrimaryPart = part

            local boxCF, boxSize = model:GetBoundingBox()
            local focus = boxCF.Position
            local maxDim = math.max(boxSize.X, boxSize.Y, boxSize.Z)
            local dist = math.max(maxDim * 1.8, 4.5)

            local cam = Instance.new("Camera", viewport)
            cam.FieldOfView = 60
            viewport.CurrentCamera = cam
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(focus + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, dist), focus)

            local base = part.CFrame
            local started = tick()
            local conn
            conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not viewport.Parent or not part.Parent then
                    if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                    return
                end
                local t = tick() - started
                part.CFrame = base * CFrame.Angles(0, t * 1.6, 0)
            end)
        end

        ctPickBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if ctPickGui then closeCtPicker(); return end
            if tick() - ctJustClosedAt < 0.25 then return end

            ctPickGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

            backdrop.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5

            frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame.ZIndex = 2
            Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
            local fStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
            fStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90)
            fStroke.Thickness = 1.2
            fStroke.Transparency = 0.3

            local titleBar = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
            titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
            titleBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 15)
            titleBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15

            local function refreshCtCards()
                for id, st in pairs(cardStrokes) do
                    local entry = nil
                    for _, e in ipairs(CUSTOM_TOOLS) do
                        if (e.cat .. "_" .. e.key) == id then entry = e; break end
                    end
                    if entry then
                        local cur = _G.AmbitiousCustomToolSkins and _G.AmbitiousCustomToolSkins[entry.cat]
                        local on = (cur == entry.key)
                        st.Color = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                        st.Thickness = on and 2 or 1
                        st.Transparency = on and 0.05 or 0.5
                    end
                end
            end

                nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -26)

                        end)

                end)

            local function refreshCards()
                for mode, st in pairs(cardStrokes) do
                    local on = (_G._KZAXTabPosition == mode)
                    st.Color = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                    st.Thickness = on and 1.8 or 1
                    st.Transparency = on and 0.05 or 0.5
                end
            end

            local MOCK = {
                Up     = {tabs={0.07,0.20,0.86,0.09}, tabsH=true,  body={0.07,0.34,0.86,0.58}},
                Bottom = {tabs={0.07,0.83,0.86,0.09}, tabsH=true,  body={0.07,0.20,0.86,0.56}},
                Left   = {tabs={0.07,0.20,0.19,0.62}, tabsH=false, body={0.31,0.20,0.62,0.72}},
                Right  = {tabs={0.74,0.20,0.19,0.62}, tabsH=false, body={0.07,0.20,0.62,0.72}},
            }

            local function drawCells(parent, x, y, w, h, n, horizontal, gap, z)
                for i = 1, n do
                    local cell = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
                    cell.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    cell.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
                    cell.BackgroundTransparency = 0.32
                    cell.ZIndex = z
                    if horizontal then
                        local cw = (w - gap*(n-1))/n
                        cell.Size = UDim2.new(cw, 0, h, 0)
                        cell.Position = UDim2.new(x + (cw+gap)*(i-1), 0, y, 0)
                    else
                        local ch = (h - gap*(n-1))/n
                        cell.Size = UDim2.new(w, 0, ch, 0)
                        cell.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, y + (ch+gap)*(i-1), 0)
                    end
                    Instance.new("UICorner", cell).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2)
                end
            end

            for i, mode in ipairs(TP_ORDER) do
                local card = Instance.new("TextButton", scrollFrame)
                card.Name = mode
                card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)
                card.BorderSizePixel = 0
                card.Text = ""
                card.AutoButtonColor = false
                card.LayoutOrder = i
                card.ZIndex = 3
                Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
                cardStrokes[mode] = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
                cardStrokes[mode].Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                cardStrokes[mode].Thickness = 1

                for j = 1, 4 do
                    local rowH = 0.115
                    local ry = M.body[2] + (rowH+0.028)*(j-1)
                    if ry + rowH > M.body[2] + M.body[4] then break end

                    r.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)

                end)

                end

            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            TS:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 404),
                Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0.5, -202)
            }):Play()
        end)

        local function updateButtonScale(delta)
            local newVal = math.clamp(buttonScaleValue + delta, 0.50, 1.50)
            if newVal ~= buttonScaleValue then
                buttonScaleValue = newVal; valueLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", buttonScaleValue)
                applyButtonScale(buttonScaleValue); saveAllSettings()
                queueConfigSave("mobile button size")
            end
        end
        leftBtn.Activated:Connect(function() updateButtonScale(-0.05) end)
        rightBtn.Activated:Connect(function() updateButtonScale(0.05) end)
        _G.buttonScaleValueLabel = valueLabel
    end
